Abstract
TheSmartParkingManagementSystemaddressescriticalchallenges
inHebron'sparkinginfrastructure,where924malfunctioningparkingmeters
createdailyfrustrationsforcitizensandenableunfairviolations.Thisproject
developsacomprehensivemobile-basedsolutionthatreplacestraditional
meterswithadigitalplatformservingdrivers,parkingenforcementofcers,
andmunicipalauthorities.
Thesystem'sprimaryobjectiveistoprovidedriverswithexiblepark-
ingmanagementthroughmobileapplications,allowingthemtostart,ex-
tend,andendparkingsessionsremotelywithoutreturningtotheirvehicles.
Adigitalwalletpaymentmodeleliminatesper-transactionfeeswhilesup-
portinglocalpaymentmethodsincludingReect,JawwalPay,andcash
deposits.Enforcementofcersverifyparkingstatusinstantlybyscanning
licenseplates,withviolationsrequiringphotographicevidenceandGPSco-
ordinatestoensurefairnessandaccountability.Municipaladministrators
monitoroperationsthroughaweb-baseddashboardprovidingreal-timean-
alytics,revenuetracking,andcomprehensivereporting.
Thearchitecturefollowsathree-layerpatternseparatingpresentation,
businesslogic,anddataaccessconcerns.Integrationwithexternalpay-
mentgateways,noticationservices,andmapprovidersenablescorefunc-
tionalitywhilemaintainingsystemmodularity.Securitymeasuresinclude
encrypteddatatransmission,securepasswordstorage,andautomaticses-
sionmanagementtoprotectuserinformation.
Expectedoutcomesincludereducedparkingsearchtime,eliminationof
unfairviolationscausedbymetermalfunctions,increasedrevenuecollec-
tionaccuracythroughtransparenttransactionrecording,andimprovedcit-
izentrustinmunicipalservices.Thesystemadaptsproveninternational
smartparkingapproachestoPalestiniantechnicalandeconomicrealities,
creatingalocallyappropriatesolutionthataddressesbothoperationalef-
ciencyandcorruptionprevention.Thisgraduationprojectdemonstrates
howmobiletechnologycanmodernizepublicserviceswhilemeetingcommunity-
specicneedswithinresourceconstraints.

1Chapter1:Introduction
ThischapterprovidesanoverviewoftheSmartParkingManagementSystem,presentingthe
coreproblemitaddressesandthemotivationbehinditsdevelopment.
1.1SystemOverview
TheSmartParkingManagementSystemisacomprehensivemobile-basedsolutionthatisde-
signedtoreplacetraditionalparkingmetersinHebroncityinPalestinethatoftenmalfunction,
withamodern,user-friendlymobileapplicationthatservesthreemainusergroups:vehicle
owners,parkingenforcementofcers,andmunicipalauthorities.
Thesystemenablesdriverstopaydigitally,managesessionsremotely,andreceivenotications.
Itprovidesenforcementofcerswithlicenseplatevericationtoolstoreduceunfairviolations,
andoffersmunicipalauthoritiesawebdashboardforreal-timemonitoringandanalytics.
Themaingoalofthissystemistosolverealproblemsthatcitizensfaceeverydaywithbroken
parkingmeters,unfairviolations,andtheinconvenienceofmanagingparkingtime.Byusing
themobiletechnologyanddigitalpayments,thesystemcreatesamorefair,anduser-friendly
parkingexperienceforeveryone.
1.2Problemstatement
ThecurrentparkingmanagementsysteminHebronfacesseveralcriticalchallengesthataffect
bothcitizensandmunicipaloperations:
1.TechnicalIssueswithPhysicalMeters:
Traditionalparkingmeterssufferfromfrequentmalfunctionsandsensorfailures,sodrivers
oftenndthemselvesreceivingviolationseventhoughtheyhavepaidandhaveremainingtime
ontheirmeters,Thesetechnicalfailurescreatefrustrationforcitizenssouserscannotreliably
dependonthephysicalequipmenttoknowtheirpaidparkingtime
2.UnfairViolationsandCorruption:
Someparkingenforcementofcerstakeadvantageofthemanualsystemtoissueunfairviola-
tions.Theydothistocollectnes,Thiscausesnancialproblemsforinnocentdriversand
makespeoplelosetrustintheparkingsystemandcreatesannegativerelationshipbetween
citizensandenforcementofcers

---PAGE---

3.LimitedFlexibilityandAccessibility:
Withtraditionalparkingmeters,youmustphysicallygobacktoyourcartopayformoretime.
Thiscreatesamajorproblem:ifyou'redelayedorneedmoretimewhileyou'reawayfromyour
car,you'reforcedtoeitherabandonwhatyou'redoingorriskgettingaviolation.Manydrivers
endupwithviolationssimplybecausetheycouldn'tphysicallyreturntotheircartoaddmore
time,eventhoughtheywouldhavebeenwillingtopayforit.
1.3Motivation
ThemotivationfordevelopingthisSmartParkingManagementSystemcomesfromboththe
needtoaddresscurrentsystemdisabilityandtheopportunitytousemoderntechnologyfor
publicbenet,plusitImprovesCitizenExperience,Citizensdeserveaparkingsystemthatis
reliable,transparent,andconvenient.Byeliminatingtheuncertaintiesassociatedwithmalfunc-
tioningmetersandprovidingtheexibilitytomanageparkingremotely,thissystemdirectly
improvesthedailylivesofdriversinHebron,Theabilitytoextendparkingtimefromanywhere
andreceiveproactivenoticationspreventsunnecessaryviolationsandreducesstressassociated
withparking.
Thesystemalsosupportsthemunicipalitybygivingcleardataandsecuretrackingforallpark-
ingactivities.Itrecordseverysession,payment,violation,andinspectoraction,whichreduces
mistakesandpreventsrevenueloss.Thedatashowswhichareasarebusyandhowpeopleuse
parking,helpingthemunicipalityplanbetterandmanagespacesmoreeasily.Thisalsoensures
thatallmoneycollectedgoestotheresponsibleauthorityinanorganizedandreliableway.

2Chapter2:BackgroundandRelatedWork
Thischapterestablishesthebroadercontextforsmartparkingsystemsbyexaminingtheglobal
parkingproblemandreviewinghowcitiesworldwidehaveaddressedsimilarchallenges.Itana-
lyzessuccessfulimplementationsinSanFrancisco,Cologne,andBarcelona,evaluatesexisting
commercialparkingapplications,andidentiesgapsthatjustifythedevelopmentofalocally
adaptedsolutionforPalestiniancities.Thechapteralsodenestheprojectscope,targetusers,
andtechnicalplatform.
2.1TheGlobalParkingProblem
Findingaparkingspothasbecomeoneofthemostfrustratingchallengesinmoderncities.
Studiesshowthatupto30%oftrafcinbusycitycenterscomesfrompeoplejustlookingfor
parking[2,3].IntheUnitedStates,driverswasteanaverageof17hourseveryyearsearching
forparkingspaces.InNewYorkCity,thisnumberjumpsto107hoursperyear[4].
Theeconomicimpactishuge.ThecostofsearchingforparkingintheU.S.reaches$73billion
annuallywhenweconsiderwastedfuel,healthcostsfromairpollution,andlostproductivity[4].
Beyondmoney,parkingproblemscontributetotrafccongestionandairpollution,whichthe
WorldHealthOrganizationlinkstoseriouspublichealthconcernsworldwide[15].
Thesechallengeshavepushedcitiesaroundtheworldtolookforbettersolutionsusingtechnol-
ogy.
2.2HowSmartParkingSystemsDeveloped
Smartparkingsystemshavecomealongwayinthepast20years.Whatstartedassimple
digitalpaymentmachineshasgrownintosophisticatedplatformsthatusesensors,mobileapps,
andarticialintelligence.Theglobalsmartparkingmarketisnowwortharound$6-7.5billion
andisgrowingbymorethan17%everyyear[11,12].Today,over1,000citiesworldwideuse
someformofsmartparkingtechnology[13].
Earlysmartparkingsystemsmainlyfocusedonmakingpaymentseasier.Modernsystemsdo
muchmore-theycandetectwhichparkingspacesareemptyinreal-time,guidedriversto
availablespotsthroughmobileapps,automaticallyrecognizelicenseplatesforenforcement,
andevenpredictwhenandwhereparkingwillbeneeded.Thesesystemstypicallyusesensors
todetectcars,communicationnetworkstosenddata(oftenusingLoRaWANtechnologythat
worksoverlongdistanceswithlowpower),mobileappsfordrivers,andanalyticssoftwarefor
parkingmanagers.

---PAGE---

2.3RealResultsfromCitiesUsingSmartParking
Severalcitieshavetestedsmartparkingsystemsandachievedimpressiveresults:
SanFrancisco'sSFparkProgram:SanFranciscowasoneoftherstU.S.citiestousesmart
parking.Thecitycutparkingsearchtimeby50%usingsensorsandadjustingpricesbasedon
demand[5,6].Whenparkingspotswerehardtond,priceswentupalittle.Whenspotswere
available,pricesdropped.Thishelpedspreadparkingmoreevenlyacrossthecity.
Cologne,Germany:Afterinstallingsmartcameras,Colognereducedparkingsearchtimeby
45%andincreasedpaymentcomplianceby44.6%[7].Thisshowshowtechnologycanhelp
bothdriversandcityrevenue.
Barcelona,Spain:Barcelona'ssmartparkingsystemgeneratedanadditional$50millionper
yearinrevenue[810].Thecitylinkedparkingmanagementtobroadergoalsformobilityand
environmentalimprovement.
Beyondrevenueandefciency,thesesystemsalsohelpedtheenvironment.Smartparking
reducesthetimedriversspendsearchingforspots,whichmeanslessfuelconsumptionand
loweremissions.StudiesshowCO2reductionsbetween20%and50%[14].
2.4WhatDriversWantfromParkingApps
Researchshowsdriversprioritizereal-timeparkingavailability,clearpricing,digitalpayments,
andaccuratenotications.However,technicalchallengesincludecameraaccuracyinpoor
weather,privacyconcerns,andtheneedforphasedimplementationtomanagecostsandaddress
issuesbeforecitywidedeployment.
2.5LookingatExistingParkingApps
Tounderstandwhat'salreadyoutthere,welookedatthreepopularparkingappsusedindifferent
partsoftheworld:
ParkMobile(UnitedStates)
ParkMobileisoneofthebiggestparkingappsinNorthAmerica.Itcoversover1million
parkingspacesin8ofthe10largestU.S.cities.
Whatworkswell:Theappiseasytouseandsendshelpfulreminders5minutesbeforeyour
parkingtimerunsout.Youcanendyourparkingsessionearlyandonlypayforthetimeyou
actuallyused.Youcansaveupto5carsinyouraccountandextendyourparkingtimefrom

---PAGE---

anywherewithoutgoingbacktoyourcar.
Problemsusersface:Everytransactioncostsanextra$0.25to$0.45,whichaddsupquickly.
Theappcrashesorhaspaymenterrorsfairlyoften.Sometimesittakeshoursforaparking
sessiontostart.Customerserviceishardtoreachandnotveryhelpful.Someusersreportbeing
chargedtwiceforthesameparkingsession.Youneedasmartphoneandinternetconnectionall
thetime.
PayByPhone(Global)
PayByPhoneisusedbyover95millionpeopleinmorethan1,300locationsaroundtheworld,
includingNorthAmericaandEurope.
Whatworkswell:Youcanpaywithmanydifferentmethods-Visa,Mastercard,PayPal,Apple
Pay,orGooglePay.Managingyourparkingfromyourphoneiseasy.Youautomaticallyget
electronicreceiptsthatareusefulforworkexpensereports.Theappworksinmanycountries.
Youcanseeallyourpastparkingsessions.
Problemsusersface:Theappcrashesorfailstoprocesspaymentsregularly.Creatingan
accountandverifyingitcanbedifcult.Youneedinternetconnectionthewholetime.Payments
sometimesfailforunclearreasons.Theapphastroubleguringoutwhereyouare.Customer
servicemainlyusesanunhelpfulchatbot,andreachingarealpersonishard.Someusersget
unexpectedchargesordoublecharges.Youcan'tchoosehowmanyminutesyouwant-only
hoursordays.
EasyPark(Europe)
EasyParkisthebiggestparkingappinEuropebycoverage.It'sbeenaroundsince2001and
worksinover4,000citiesacrossmorethan20countries.
Whatworkswell:ItworksalmosteverywhereinEurope.Youonlypayfortheexacttimeyou
park.Ithasadvancedfeatureslikendingelectriccarchargingstations.Youcanmanagetwo
parkingsessionsatthesametime.Addingtemporarycarslikerentalsiseasy.Thecompanyhas
over20yearsofexperience.
Problemsusersface:Servicefeescanreach15%insomecountries,whichisexpensive.The
feesaren'talwaysclearupfront.Theapphastechnicalproblemsandglitches.Theadvanced
featuresarecomplicatedforpeoplewhojustwantbasicparking.Becausefeesareapercentage
ofwhatyoupay,costsaddupovertime.

2.6GapAnalysisandProjectJustication
Whilecommercialparkingappsprovethatmobilesystemswork,theypresentchallengesfor
thePalestiniancontext:
CostStructure:Majorplatformschargetransactionfeesortakepercentagesfromeachpay-
ment,makingparkingexpensiveforregularusers.Inareaswithlimitedcreditcardaccess,these
feescreatebarrierstoadoption.
InfrastructureDependencies:Existingappsassumereliableinternet,widespreadsmartphone
ownership,andinternationalpaymentsystemslikePayPalorcreditcards.Whilesmartphones
arecommoninPalestine,paymentinfrastructureandconnectivitydonotfullymatchthese
assumptions.
LimitedCustomization:Commercialplatformscannoteasilyadapttolocalpaymentmethods
likeReectandJawwalPay,ortospecicPalestinianmunicipalregulationsandenforcement
procedures.
CorruptionPrevention:Existingsystemsimproveefciencybutwerenotdesignedtoaddress
corruption,whichisakeychallengeinPalestinianparkingmanagement.
TheproposedSmartParkingManagementSystemaddressesthesegapsthroughtargeteddesign
decisions.Itusesawallet-basedpaymentmodelwhereusersloadfundsonceformultipleses-
sions,eliminatingper-transactionfees.ThesystemintegrateslocalpaymentmethodsPalestini-
ansactuallyuseReect,JawwalPay,andcashdepositsatmunicipalofces.Everytransaction
isrecordedandtraceabletopreventcorruptionandensureaccountability.Thearchitectureis
designedforaffordabilityandcustomizationtomeetPalestinianmunicipalneedsandbudget
constraints.
Whilethesystemrequiresinternetaccessforcoreoperations,ithandlesintermittentorlow-
qualityconnectionsbetterthancommercialalternativesthroughlocaldatacachingandasyn-
chronoussynchronization.
ThisprojectadaptsproveninternationalsmartparkingbenetstoPalestiniantechnical,eco-
nomic,andoperationalrealities,creatinganeffectiveandpracticalsolutionforlocaldeploy-
ment.

2.7ScopeofProject
2.7.1TargetUsers
TheSmartParkingManagementSystemisdesignedtoservethreedistinctusergroupsinHe-
broncity:
VehicleOwners/Drivers:Theprimaryuserswhoneedtopayforparkingandmanagetheir
parkingsessionsthroughamobileapplication.Thesystemallowsthemtostartandendparking
sessionsdigitally,extendtheirparkingtimeremotelywithoutreturningtotheirvehicle,receive
noticationsbeforetheirparkingexpires,viewtheircompleteparkingandpaymenthistory,and
manageanyviolationstheyreceive.
ParkingEnforcementOfcers(Inspectors):Ofcerswhomonitorparkingcomplianceinthe
eld.Thesystemprovidesthemwithtoolstoscanvehiclelicenseplatesandinstantlyverifyif
parkingispaidorexpired,issuedigitalviolationswithphotoevidenceandGPSlocation,record
clampedvehicles,andtracktheirdailyenforcementactivities.
MunicipalAuthorities:Cityofcialswhooverseetheentireparkingsystemthroughaweb-
baseddashboard.Theycanmonitorallparkingoperationsandrevenueinreal-time,viewde-
tailedreportsandstatisticsonparkingusagepatterns,managesystemsettingsincludingparking
ratesandtimelimits,overseeenforcementactivitiesandviolationrecords,andusedataanalyt-
icstomakeinformeddecisionsaboutparkingpoliciesandinfrastructureplanning.
2.7.2CoreFeatures
Thesystemincludesthefollowingkeyfeatures:
DigitalWalletPaymentSystem:Usersloadmoneyintotheirdigitalwalletusinglocalpay-
mentmethods(Reect,JawwalPay)orcashdepositsatmunicipalityofces.Thewalletbalance
isthenusedtopayforparkingsessions,eliminatingper-transactionfeesandsupportingusers
whodon'thavecreditcards.Thisapproachsignicantlyreducescostscomparedtointerna-
tionalparkingappsthatchargefeesforeverytransaction.
MobileParkingSessions:Driverscanstart,extend,andendparkingsessionsdirectlyfrom
theirsmartphoneswithoutphysicalinteractionwithparkingmeters.ThesystemusesQRcodes
placedatparkingspotstoinitiatesessions,capturesGPSlocationautomatically,andallows
exibletimeextensionsfromanywhere.
Real-TimeParkingMap:Aninteractivemapdisplaysoccupiedparkingspotsthroughoutthe
city.Themapshowsvehiclesparkedthroughtheapp(markedinblue)andclampedvehicles

---PAGE---

(markedinred).Bothdriversandinspectorscanviewthismaptoseeparkingavailabilityand
enforcementstatusinreal-time.
AutomatedNotications:Thesystemsendspushnoticationstodrivers5minutesbefore
theirparkingsessionexpires,whentheirsessionends,whentheyreceiveaviolation,andwhen
paymentisdue.Thisproactivecommunicationhelpspreventunnecessaryviolations.
LicensePlateVerication:Inspectorsusetheirmobileapptoscanvehiclelicenseplates(either
bytypingtheplatenumberorusingcamerarecognitioniftechnicallyfeasible).Thesystem
instantlyshowswhetherthevehiclehasanactiveparkingsessionorifparkinghasexpired,
eliminatingguessworkandreducingunfairviolations.
DigitalViolationManagement:Wheninspectorsissueviolations,thesystemrequiresphoto
evidence,automaticallyrecordsGPSlocationandtimestamp,trackstheviolationthroughpay-
mentandresolution,andmaintainsacompleteaudittrail.Forclampedvehicles,thesystem
addsthelocationtothemapandtrackswhentheclampisremovedafterpayment.
ComprehensiveDashboard:Municipalauthoritiesaccessaweb-baseddashboardthatdis-
playsreal-timeparkingoccupancy,revenuetracking,violationstatistics,inspectoractivitylogs,
andcustomizablereports.Thedashboardprovidesvisualanalyticstohelpidentifybusyparking
areasandusagepatterns.
TransactionTransparency:Everyactioninthesystem-parkingsessions,payments,viola-
tions,andinspectoractivities-isrecordedwithtimestamps,userIDs,andlocationdata.This
createscompleteaccountabilityandhelpspreventcorruptionbymakingallactivitiestraceable
andauditable.
2.7.3TechnicalPlatform
Mobile:Fluttercross-platformdevelopmentforAndroid/iOSdriverandinspectorapps.Web:
Modernbrowsers,responsivedesign,Node.jsbackend.Backend:RESTfulAPI,Node.js,Web-
Socketforreal-timeupdates.Technologies:QRcodes,GPS,pushnotications,paymentgate-
ways,mapservices(GoogleMaps/OpenStreetMap).
2.7.4Limitations
Thefollowingfeaturesareexcludedfromtheinitialdeploymentandmaybeconsideredfor
futureversions:
AdvancedNavigationFeatures:Directionstoparkingspots,routeoptimization,andintegra-
tionwithnavigationapps.

---PAGE---

GovernmentSystemIntegration:Connectionstotrafcmanagement,vehicleregistration
databases,orothermunicipalservices.
ParkingReservations:Pre-bookingspecicparkingspotsinadvance.
DynamicPricing:Automaticrateadjustmentsbasedondemand,time,orlocation(manual
congurationissupported).
Multi-CityDeployment:ThesystemisdesignedforHebroncity;scalingtomultiplecities
requiresadditionaldevelopment.
OfineMode:Thesystemrequiresinternetconnectivityforcoreoperations.Fullofinefunc-
tionalityisnotsupported,thoughsomebasicfeatureslikeviewinghistorymaycachelocally.
Thisscopedenesarealisticandimplementablesystemthataddressesthecoreproblemsin
Hebron'sparkingmanagementwhileremainingfeasiblewithinagraduationprojecttimeline
andavailableresources.

3.2Non-functionalRequirements
3.2.1Usability
Thesystemshallprovideaninteractive,intuitive,andeasy-to-useinterfacethatenablesusers
ofalltechnicalskilllevelstocompletecoretasksefciently.
Metrics:
Newusersshallcompleteregistrationandstarttheirrstparkingsessionwithin5minutes
withoutassistance
Atleast80%ofusersintestingsessionsshallsuccessfullycompletecoretasksontheir
rstattempt
TheinterfaceshallachieveaSystemUsabilityScale(SUS)scoreofatleast70
MeasurementMethod:Usertestingsessionswithrepresentativeusersfromeachgroup(drivers,
inspectors,administrators).
3.2.2Performance
Thesystemshallensurefastresponsetimesforallcriticaloperationstopreventdelaysthat
couldresultinparkingviolations.
Metrics:

---PAGE---

Startingaparkingsession:Responsetime<2secondsundernormalload
Processingpayments:Transactioncompletion<3seconds
Extendingparkingtime:Responsetime<2seconds
Pushnoticationdelivery:<5secondsfromtriggerevent
Systemshallmaintainresponsetimesduringpeakusage(500+concurrentusers)
Systemuptime:99%availabilityduringoperatinghours
MeasurementMethod:Loadtestingusingautomatedtestingtoolssimulatingconcurrentusers
andmonitoringresponsetimesandsystemresourceusage.
3.2.3Security
Thesystemshallimplementcomprehensivesecuritymeasurestoprotectsensitiveuserdataand
paymentinformation.
SecurityRequirements:
Allcommunicationbetweenmobileapplicationsandservershallbeencryptedtoprevent
datainterception
Paymentprocessingshallusecertiedthird-partypaymentgatewaysthatcomplywith
internationalsecuritystandards
Userpasswordsshallbestoredusingsecureencryptionmethodsthatcannotbereversed
Usersessionsshallautomaticallyexpireafter30minutesofinactivitytopreventunautho-
rizedaccess
Thesystemshalllimitloginattemptsto5triesbeforetemporarilylockingtheaccountfor
15minutes
MeasurementMethod:Securitytestingtoverifyencryption,passwordprotection,anddefense
againstcommonsecuritythreats.

4Chapter4:SystemDesign
ThischapterpresentsthearchitecturaldesignandtechnicalspecicationsoftheSmartPark-
ingManagementSystem.Itexplainstherationaleforselectingalayeredarchitecturepattern
anddetailsthesystem'sstructuralcomponentsacrossthreedistinctlayers.Thechapterthen
illustratesthedatamodelthroughanentity-relationshipdiagramthatdenesthesystem'score
entitiesandtheirrelationships.Finally,itshowsuserinterfacemockupsforthethreemainuser
groups:drivers,inspectors,andadministrators.
4.1SystemArchitecture
TheSmartParkingManagementSystemfollowsaLayeredArchitecturepatterntoensureclear
separationofconcerns,maintainability,andscalability.Thisarchitecturesuitstheproject's
needsgiventhethreedistinctusertypes(drivers,inspectors,andadministrators),eachrequiring
separateinterfaces.
Thearchitectureconsistsofthreemainlayers:
PresentationLayer:Handlesuserinteractionsthroughmobileapplicationsfordriversand
inspectors,andaweb-baseddashboardforadministrators.
BusinessLogicLayer:Containscorefunctionalityincludingsessionmanagement,violation
processing,paymenthandling,noticationdistribution,andreportgeneration.Thislayeralso
managescommunicationwithexternalservices.
DataAccessLayer:Managesalldatabaseoperations,ensuringdataconsistencyandintegrity
whileabstractingdatabaseinteractionsfrombusinesslogic.
ExternalServices:Thesystemintegrateswithpaymentgateways,noticationservices,and
mapproviders.TheseservicescommunicatedirectlywiththeBusinessLogicLayer.
Thisstructureallowsindependentdevelopment,testing,andmaintenanceofeachcomponent
whileensuringsecurecommunicationthroughwell-denedinterfaces.
